> 148	## Video Rescue Protocol
> 149	
> 150	When a video generation operation (Google Veo) times out or client disconnects:
> 151	```bash
> 152	# Via edge function (production):
> 153	curl -X POST "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/rescue-video" \
> 154	  -H "Authorization: Bearer <user_jwt>" \
> 155	  -d '{"content_id":"<uuid>","operation_id":"<operation_name>"}'
> 156	
> 157	# Via local script (admin/debug):
> 158	source .env && bun run rescue.ts <operation_id> <content_id>
> 159	```

> 195	### Canvas State Architecture (Hybrid)
> 196	
> 197	```
> 198	User interaction → ReactFlow internal state (positions, drag)
> 199	                         ↓ (debounced 600ms)
> 200	                   localStorage (positions persist across refresh)
> 201	                         ↑
> 202	Store (Zustand) ← runPipeline → orchestrate-content Edge Function
> 203	     ↓ (status changes sync to ReactFlow via useEffect)
> 204	ReactFlow renders visual status (idle/running/done/error/blocked)
> 205	```

> 151	              exit={{ x: '100%', opacity: 0 }}

> 155	              <MemoryGraphWidget />
> 156	            </motion.div>
> 157	          )}

> 13	interface AgentEdgeProps {

> 15	  toPos: [number, number, number];
> 16	  status: Edge['status'];
> 17	  color?: string;
> 18	}
> 19	
> 20	export function AgentEdge({ fromPos, toPos, status, color: customColor }: AgentEdgeProps) {
> 21	  const lineRef = useRef<THREE.Line>(null!);
> 22	  const particlesRef = useRef<THREE.Points>(null!);
> 23	  const color = customColor || EDGE_COLORS[status];
> 24	
> 25	  const curve = useMemo(() => {
> 26	    const from = new THREE.Vector3(...fromPos);
> 27	    const to = new THREE.Vector3(...toPos);
> 28	    
> 29	    // Add a slight arc to make it look less linear and more biological/constellation-like
> 30	    const mid = from.clone().add(to).multiplyScalar(0.5);
> 31	    const dist = from.distanceTo(to);
> 32	    mid.y += dist * 0.2; // Arc height based on distance
> 33	    mid.z += (Math.random() - 0.5) * dist * 0.1;
> 34	    
> 35	    return new THREE.QuadraticBezierCurve3(from, mid, to);
> 36	  }, [fromPos, toPos]);
> 37	
> 38	  const lineGeom = useMemo(() => {
> 39	    const points = curve.getPoints(50);
> 40	    return new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
> 41	  }, [curve]);
> 42	
> 43	  // Higher density particle system for "data transfer"
> 44	  const particleCount = status === 'active' ? 24 : 10;
> 45	  const particlePositions = useMemo(() => new Float32Array(particleCount * 3), [particleCount]);
> 46	  const offsets = useMemo(() => Array.from({ length: particleCount }, (_, i) => i / particleCount), [particleCount]);
> 47	
> 48	  useFrame(({ clock }) => {
> 49	    if (!particlesRef.current) return;
> 50	    const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
> 51	    const speed = status === 'active' ? 0.8 : status === 'waiting' ? 0.3 : 0.1;
> 52	
> 53	    for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
> 54	      const progress = (offsets[i] + t * speed) % 1;
> 55	      const pt = curve.getPointAt(progress);
> 56	      particlePositions[i * 3] = pt.x;
> 57	      particlePositions[i * 3 + 1] = pt.y;
> 58	      particlePositions[i * 3 + 2] = pt.z;
> 59	    }
> 60	    particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
> 61	    
> 62	    if (lineRef.current) {
> 63	      // Pulsating opacity for the connection
> 64	      (lineRef.current.material as THREE.LineBasicMaterial).opacity = 
> 65	        (status === 'idle' ? 0.1 : 0.3) + Math.sin(t * 4) * 0.1;
> 66	    }
> 67	  });
> 68	
> 69	  return (
> 70	    <group>
> 71	      {/* Background Glow Path */}
> 72	      {/* @ts-ignore */}
> 73	      <line ref={lineRef} geometry={lineGeom}>
> 74	        <lineBasicMaterial 
> 75	          color={color} 
> 76	          transparent 
> 77	          opacity={0.3} 
> 78	          linewidth={2} 
> 79	          blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
> 80	        />
> 81	      </line>
> 82	      
> 83	      {/* Moving Data Packets */}
> 84	      <points ref={particlesRef}>
> 85	        <bufferGeometry>
> 86	          <bufferAttribute
> 87	            attach="attributes-position"
> 88	            array={particlePositions}
> 89	            count={particleCount}
> 90	            itemSize={3}
> 91	          />
> 92	        </bufferGeometry>
> 93	        <pointsMaterial 
> 94	          color={color} 
> 95	          size={status === 'active' ? 0.4 : 0.25} 
> 96	          transparent 
> 97	          opacity={0.9} 
> 98	          sizeAttenuation 
> 99	          blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
> 100	          depthWrite={false}
> 101	        />
> 102	      </points>
> 103	
> 104	      {/* Subtle additional glow for active edges */}
> 105	      {status === 'active' && (
> 106	        <points>
> 107	          <bufferGeometry>
> 108	            <bufferAttribute
> 109	              attach="attributes-position"
> 110	              array={particlePositions}
> 111	              count={particleCount}
> 112	              itemSize={3}
> 113	            />
> 114	          </bufferGeometry>
> 115	          <pointsMaterial 
> 116	            color={color} 
> 117	            size={0.6} 
> 118	            transparent 
> 119	            opacity={0.1} 
> 120	            sizeAttenuation 
> 121	            blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
> 122	            depthWrite={false}
> 123	          />
> 124	        </points>
> 125	      )}
> 126	    </group>
> 127	  );
> 128	}
> 129	
> ```

## 🤖 Claude *(15:10:43)*

Build limpo, sem erros. Três correções aplicadas:

---

**Fix 1 — `useCrewData.ts`** (spinner infinito)
`setLoaded(true)` agora é chamado também quando `edgesQuery.isError = true`. Antes, se a query do Supabase falhasse (403, 404, network error), `data` ficava `undefined`, o `if (edgesQuery.data)` nunca passava, e o spinner não saía nunca.

**Fix 2 — `AgentEdge.tsx`** (THREE.js crash)
Adicionado `key={particleCount}` no elemento `<points>`. O WebGL não suporta redimensionar um `BufferAttribute` in-place (de 24→10 partículas quando `status` muda entre `active` e `idle`). Com o `key`, o React desmonta e remonta o elemento criando um novo buffer GPU do zero.

**Fix 3 — `SquadHull.tsx`** (font 404)
Removido `font="/fonts/Inter-Bold.woff"`. O arquivo não existe em `public/fonts/`. O componente `Text` do drei usa a fonte padrão (Roboto via CDN) sem o prop — nenhuma dependência local, sem erro de carga.

> 44

> 135	
> 136	      velocities.current[i3] += (targets.current[i3] - dummy.position.x) * spring;
> 137	      velocities.current[i3 + 1] += (targets.current[i3 + 1] - dummy.position.y) * spring;
> 138	      velocities.current[i3 + 2] += (targets.current[i3 + 2] - dummy.position.z) * spring;
> 139	
> 140	      velocities.current[i3] *= damping;
> 141	      velocities.current[i3 + 1] *= damping;
> 142	      velocities.current[i3 + 2] *= damping;
> 143	
> 144	      dummy.position.x += velocities.current[i3];
> 145	      dummy.position.y += velocities.current[i3 + 1];
> 146	      dummy.position.z += velocities.current[i3 + 2];
> 147	
> 148	      // Scale based on gesture
> 149	      const baseScale = handData.isActive ? 0.04 + Math.sin(time * 3 + i) * 0.015 : 0.025;
> 150	      dummy.scale.setScalar(baseScale);
> 151	
> 152	      dummy.updateMatrix();
> 153	      meshRef.current.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
> 154	    }
> 155	
> 156	    meshRef.current.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
> 157	
> 158	    // Update color for all instances
> 159	    const mat = meshRef.current.material as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
> 160	    mat.color.copy(colorRef.current);
> 161	  });
> 162	
> 163	  return (
> 164	    <instancedMesh ref={meshRef} args={[undefined, undefined, PARTICLE_COUNT]}>
> 165	      <sphereGeometry args={[1, 8, 8]} />
> 166	      <meshBasicMaterial transparent opacity={0.8} toneMapped={false} />
> 167	    </instancedMesh>
> 168	  );
> 169	}
> 170	
> 171	function HandCursor({ handData }: { handData: HandData }) {
> 172	  const meshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
> 173	  const ringRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
> 174	
> 175	  useFrame((state) => {
> 176	    if (!meshRef.current || !handData.isActive) return;
> 177	
> 178	    const x = (handData.indexTip.x - 0.5) * -8;
> 179	    const y = (handData.indexTip.y - 0.5) * -6;
> 180	
> 181	    meshRef.current.position.x += (x - meshRef.current.position.x) * 0.2;
> 182	    meshRef.current.position.y += (y - meshRef.current.position.y) * 0.2;
> 183	    meshRef.current.position.z = 2;
> 184	
> 185	    const scale = handData.gesture === 'pinch' ? 0.15 : 0.1;
> 186	    meshRef.current.scale.setScalar(scale);
> 187	
> 188	    if (ringRef.current) {
> 189	      ringRef.current.position.copy(meshRef.current.position);
> 190	      ringRef.current.rotation.z = state.clock.elapsedTime;
> 191	      const ringScale = handData.gesture === 'open_palm' ? 0.5 : 0.3;
> 192	      ringRef.current.scale.setScalar(ringScale);
> 193	    }
> 194	  });
> 195	
> 196	  if (!handData.isActive) return null;
> 197	
> 198	  return (
> 199	    <>
> 200	      <mesh ref={meshRef}>
> 201	        <sphereGeometry args={[1, 16, 16]} />
> 202	        <meshBasicMaterial color="#38bdf8" transparent opacity={0.6} toneMapped={false} />
> 203	      </mesh>
> 204	      <mesh ref={ringRef}>
> 205	        <torusGeometry args={[1, 0.05, 8, 32]} />
> 206	        <meshBasicMaterial color="#a855f7" transparent opacity={0.4} toneMapped={false} />
> 207	      </mesh>
> 208	    </>
> 209	  );
> 210	}
> 211	
> 212	export function GestureParticleOverlay({ handData }: { handData: HandData }) {
> 213	  return (
> 214	    <div className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none z-40">
> 215	      <Canvas
> 216	        camera={{ position: [0, 0, 8], fov: 50 }}
> 217	        gl={{ alpha: true, antialias: true }}
> 218	        style={{ background: 'transparent' }}
> 219	      >
> 220	        <Particles handData={handData} />
> 221	        <HandCursor handData={handData} />
> 222	      </Canvas>
> 223	    </div>
> 224	  );
> 225	}
> 226	
> ```

> 6	interface CreateAgentInput {

> 10	  avatar: string;
> 11	  provider: string;

> 24	  model?: string;

> 29	
> 30	export function useCreateAgent() {
> 31	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 32	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 33	
> 34	  return useMutation({
> 35	    mutationFn: async (input: CreateAgentInput) => {
> 36	      const { error } = await supabase.from('crew_agents').insert({
> 37	        user_id: user!.id,

> 43	    onSuccess: () => {

> 1	import { Canvas } from '@react-three/fiber';
> 2	import { OrbitControls, Stars, Float } from '@react-three/drei';
> 3	import { EffectComposer, Bloom, ChromaticAberration, Noise, Vignette } from '@react-three/postprocessing';
> 4	import { useCrewStore } from '@/store/useCrewStore';
> 5	import { AgentNode } from './AgentNode';
> 6	import { AgentEdge } from './AgentEdge';
> 7	import { SquadHull } from './SquadHull';
> 8	import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
> 9	import * as THREE from 'three';
> 10	
> 11	function Scene() {
> 12	  const agents = useCrewStore((s) => s.agents || []);
> 13	  const edges = useCrewStore((s) => s.edges || []);
> 14	  const selectedAgentId = useCrewStore((s) => s.selectedAgentId);
> 15	  const setSelectedAgent = useCrewStore((s) => s.setSelectedAgent);
> 16	  const connectionMode = useCrewStore((s) => s.connectionMode);
> 17	  const connectingFrom = useCrewStore((s) => s.connectingFrom);
> 18	  const setConnectingFrom = useCrewStore((s) => s.setConnectingFrom);
> 19	
> 20	  const agentMap = useMemo(() => {
> 21	    const map = new Map();
> 22	    if (agents && Array.isArray(agents)) {
> 23	      agents.forEach(a => {
> 24	        if (a && a.id) map.set(a.id, a);
> 25	      });
> 26	    }
> 27	    return map;
> 28	  }, [agents]);
> 29	
> 30	  const squads = useMemo(() => {
> 31	    if (!agents || !Array.isArray(agents)) return [];
> 32	    const unique = new Set(agents.map(a => a?.squad).filter(Boolean));
> 33	    return Array.from(unique) as string[];
> 34	  }, [agents]);
> 35	
> 36	  const SQUAD_META: Record<string, { name: string, color: string }> = {
> 37	    content: { name: 'Creative Content', color: '#ec4899' },
> 38	    tech: { name: 'Technical Engine', color: '#3b82f6' },
> 39	    market: { name: 'Market Intelligence', color: '#22c55e' },
> 40	    ops: { name: 'Operational Ops', color: '#f59e0b' },
> 41	  };
> 42	
> 43	  const handleNodeClick = useCallback((agentId: string) => {
> 44	    if (connectionMode) {
> 45	      if (!connectingFrom) {
> 46	        setConnectingFrom(agentId);
> 47	      } else if (connectingFrom !== agentId) {
> 48	        (window as any).__openEdgeDialog?.(connectingFrom, agentId);
> 49	      } else {
> 50	        setConnectingFrom(null);
> 51	      }
> 52	    } else {
> 53	      setSelectedAgent(agentId === selectedAgentId ? null : agentId);
> 54	    }
> 55	  }, [connectionMode, connectingFrom, selectedAgentId, setSelectedAgent, setConnectingFrom]);
> 56	
> 57	  return (
> 58	    <>
> 59	      <color attach="background" args={['#000205']} />
> 60	      <fog attach="fog" args={['#000205', 10, 40]} />
> 61	      <ambientLight intensity={0.1} />
> 62	      <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} intensity={3} color="#38bdf8" />
> 63	      <pointLight position={[-10, -5, -10]} intensity={2} color="#a855f7" />
> 64	      <pointLight position={[0, 10, -20]} intensity={2} color="#22d3ee" />
> 65	      
> 66	      <Stars radius={150} depth={60} count={12000} factor={7} saturation={0.5} fade speed={2} />
> 67	      
> 68	      {/* Dynamic Grid Floor */}
> 69	      <gridHelper args={[100, 100, '#1e293b', '#080d1a']} position={[0, -8, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, 0]} />
> 70	
> 71	      <group>
> 72	        {/* Render Squad Hulls */}
> 73	        {squads && squads.map(squadId => (
> 74	          <SquadHull 
> 75	            key={squadId} 
> 76	            squadId={squadId} 
> 77	            name={SQUAD_META[squadId]?.name || squadId}
> 78	            color={SQUAD_META[squadId]?.color || '#ffffff'}
> 79	          />
> 80	        ))}
> 81	
> 82	        {edges && edges.map((edge) => {
> 83	          const fromAgent = agentMap.get(edge.from);
> 84	          const toAgent = agentMap.get(edge.to);
> 85	          if (!fromAgent?.position || !toAgent?.position) return null;
> 86	          return (
> 87	            <AgentEdge
> 88	              key={edge.id}
> 89	              fromPos={fromAgent.position}
> 90	              toPos={toAgent.position}
> 91	              status={edge.status}
> 92	              color={fromAgent.squad === toAgent.squad ? (SQUAD_META[fromAgent.squad || '']?.color || '#3b82f6') : '#ffffff'}
> 93	            />
> 94	          );
> 95	        })}
> 96	
> 97	        {agents && agents.map((agent) => (
> 98	          <Float key={agent.id} speed={1.5} rotationIntensity={0.5} floatIntensity={0.5}>
> 99	            <AgentNode
> 100	              agent={agent}
> 101	              selected={selectedAgentId === agent.id}
> 102	              connecting={connectionMode && connectingFrom === agent.id}
> 103	              connectionMode={connectionMode}
> 104	              onClick={() => handleNodeClick(agent.id)}
> 105	            />
> 106	          </Float>
> 107	        ))}
> 108	      </group>
> 109	
> 110	      <EffectComposer>
> 111	        <Bloom 
> 112	          intensity={2.8} 
> 113	          luminanceThreshold={0.1} 
> 114	          luminanceSmoothing={0.7} 
> 115	          mipmapBlur 
> 116	        />
> 117	        <ChromaticAberration offset={new THREE.Vector2(0.002, 0.002)} radialModulation={true} modulationOffset={0.5} />
> 118	        <Noise opacity={0.12} />
> 119	        <Vignette eskil={false} offset={0.05} darkness={1.4} />
> 120	      </EffectComposer>
> 121	    </>
> 122	  );
> 123	}
> 124	
> 125	export function CrewGraph() {
> 126	  return (
> 127	    <div className="absolute inset-0">
> 128	      <Canvas
> 129	        camera={{ position: [0, 5, 15], fov: 45 }}
> 130	        gl={{ antialias: true, alpha: true, stencil: false, depth: true }}
> 131	        style={{ background: '#020617' }}
> 132	      >
> 133	        <Scene />
> 134	        <OrbitControls
> 135	          enableDamping
> 136	          dampingFactor={0.05}
> 137	          minDistance={5}
> 138	          maxDistance={35}
> 139	          maxPolarAngle={Math.PI / 1.5}
> 140	          minPolarAngle={Math.PI / 6}
> 141	        />
> 142	      </Canvas>
> 143	    </div>
> 144	  );
> 145	}

## 🤖 Claude *(15:20:51)*

Localizei o bug. Na biblioteca `@react-three/postprocessing` v3.0.4, o `useLayoutEffect` do `EffectComposer` re-executa toda vez que os filhos mudam de referência. O `ChromaticAberration offset={new THREE.Vector2(...)}` cria um objeto novo a cada render do `Scene` → dispara o `useLayoutEffect` → `__r3f.children` pode estar `undefined` durante o commit parcial → crash loop → WebGL Context Lost.

Dois fixes: extrair o `EffectComposer` em `React.memo` (sem props = sem re-renders), e adicionar o null-guard na lib.

Stable memo component eliminates the race.\nconst PostProcessing = memo(function PostProcessing() {\n  const chromaticOffset = useMemo(() => new THREE.Vector2(0.002, 0.002), []);\n  return (\n    <EffectComposer>\n      <Bloom intensity={2.8} luminanceThreshold={0.1} luminanceSmoothing={0.7} mipmapBlur />\n      <ChromaticAberration offset={chromaticOffset} radialModulation={true} modulationOffset={0.5} />\n      <Noise opacity={0.12} />\n      <Vignette eskil={false} offset={0.05} darkness={1.4} />\n    </EffectComposer>\n  );\n});"

## 🤖 Claude *(15:23:17)*

Build limpo. Hash do ConstellationPage mudou (`CaZrufU7`), confirmando o fix incluído.

---

**Resumo dos 3 fixes desta sessão:**

**Fix 3 (novo) — `CrewGraph.tsx`** — Causa raiz do crash + WebGL Context Lost  
`EffectComposer` extraído para componente `PostProcessing` com `React.memo`. O bug era: a cada re-render do `Scene` (causado por mudança de agentes), `new THREE.Vector2(0.002, 0.002)` criava nova referência → `children` prop do `EffectComposer` mudava → `useLayoutEffect` interno re-disparava → durante o commit parcial do R3F, `__r3f.children` estava `undefined` → `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'length')` → loop de erros → `THREE.WebGLRenderer: Context Lost`.

Com `memo` sem props, o `EffectComposer` nunca re-renderiza por causa de mudanças de agentes — o `useLayoutEffect` só executa uma vez (no mount), quando o R3F já terminou de popular `__r3f.children`.

O `social-auth-init` 500 é erro separado no edge function de OAuth — não relacionado à Constelação. Quer que eu investigue esse também?

## 🤖 Claude *(15:29:14)*


## 🤖 Claude *(15:29:15)*

**Achei a causa raiz!** É uma incompatibilidade de versão. O R3F v8 usa `__r3f.objects` para filhos não-Object3D, mas o `@react-three/postprocessing` v3.0.4 lê `__r3f.children` (que não existe no R3F v8 = `undefined`).

## 🤖 Claude *(15:31:48)*

`__r3f.children` zerado no bundle. O crash foi eliminado na origem.

---

**Diagnóstico final:**

A causa raiz era uma **incompatibilidade de versão crítica**:
- `@react-three/postprocessing` **v3.0.4** foi escrita para R3F **v9+**
- Internamente ela lia `group.__r3f.children` para achar os effects filhos
- Mas o R3F **v8.18** (que está no projeto) não tem `__r3f.children` — usa `__r3f.objects`
- Resultado: `undefined.length` → crash loop a 60fps → WebGL Context Lost → canvas destruído

**Fix aplicado:**
1. Downgrade `@react-three/postprocessing` de `3.0.4` → **`2.19.1`** (última v2.x, compatível com R3F v8)
2. Removidos props `radialModulation` e `modulationOffset` do `ChromaticAberration` que eram exclusivos da v3 API
3. `PostProcessing` continua como `memo()` para evitar re-renders desnecessários

O `package.json` foi atualizado automaticamente pelo `bun add`. Pode fazer o deploy agora.

## 👤 User *(15:35:39)*

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - User started session with `/handson` to load context from HANDOFF.md and knowledge mesh
   - User reported the **Constellation page** (`/dashboard/constellation`) was stuck in an infinite loading spinner with browser error: `Failure loading font https://login.mcorch.com/fonts/Inter-Bold.woff`
   - After first fixes, user reported new crash logs: `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'length')` at `ConstellationPage-C59COjPA.js:476:5412` + `THREE.WebGLRenderer: Context Lost`
   - User confirmed the page was still broken after the `React.memo` fix attempt: "Ainda não vejo a pagina de constelação. Precisamos resolver, consulte os logs."
   - User executed `/handoff` to seal the session with commits, HANDOFF.md update, and milestone node insert

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **React Three Fiber (R3F) v8.18** — 3D scene renderer; uses `__r3f.objects` for non-Object3D children (NOT `__r3f.children` which is R3F v9 API)
   - **@react-three/postprocessing v3.0.4 vs v2.x** — v3 requires R3F `^9.0.0`; reads `__r3f.children` (undefined in R3F v8) causing crash
   - **THREE.js `WebGLAttributes` buffer resize** — WebGL buffers cannot be resized in-place; changing `BufferAttribute` array length crashes renderer
   - **TanStack Query `isSuccess`/`isError`** — correct flags to use vs raw `data` truthiness for settled state detection
   - **Zustand `loaded` state** — ConstellationPage spinner gated on `loaded && !crewLoading`; never cleared if query errors
   - **R3F `__r3f.objects` vs `__r3f.children`** — R3F v8 uses `objects` array for non-Object3D children; postprocessing v3 incorrectly reads `children`
   - **`React.memo`** — prevents re-renders of EffectComposer from parent state changes (kept as secondary protection)
   - **`postprocessing` library v6.39.1** — underlying effects library; `ChromaticAberrationEffect` in v2.x doesn't support `radialModulation`/`modulationOffset` (v3 API only)

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/hooks/useCrewData.ts`**
     - Critical: `setLoaded(true)` was only called inside `if (edgesQuery.data)` — never called when query errored, causing infinite spinner
     - Fix: replaced with `isSuccess`/`isError` check:
     ```typescript
     useEffect(() => {
       if (edgesQuery.isSuccess) {
         setEdges(edgesQuery.data);
         setLoaded(true);
       } else if (edgesQuery.isError) {
         setLoaded(true);
       }
     }, [edgesQuery.isSuccess, edgesQuery.isError, edgesQuery.data, setEdges, setLoaded]);
     ```

   - **`src/components/graph/AgentEdge.tsx`**
     - `particleCount` changes between 24 (active) and 10 (idle) based on edge status
     - THREE.js throws `WebGLAttributes: The size of the buffer attribute's array buffer does not match the original size` when resizing in-place
     - Fix: added `key={particleCount}` to force React to unmount+remount `<points>` creating fresh GPU buffer:
     ```tsx
     {/* Moving Data Packets — key forces remount when buffer size changes (active: 24 vs idle: 10) */}
     <points ref={particlesRef} key={particleCount}>
     ```

   - **`src/components/graph/SquadHull.tsx`**
     - Had `font="/fonts/Inter-Bold.woff"` on drei's `Text` component but `public/fonts/` directory doesn't exist
     - Loading failure caused `Failure loading font https://login.mcorch.com/fonts/Inter-Bold.woff` error
     - Fix: removed `font` prop entirely; drei uses default Roboto font from CDN:
     ```tsx
     <Text
       position={[0, radius + 1.5, 0]}
       fontSize={0.6}
       color={color}
       anchorX="center"
       anchorY="middle"
     >
     ```

   - **`src/components/graph/CrewGraph.tsx`**
     - Root cause: `@react-three/postprocessing` v3.0.4 required R3F v9 API (`__r3f.children`) but project uses R3F v8.18 (`__r3f.objects`) — `__r3f.children` was always `undefined` → crash loop at 60fps → WebGL Context Lost
     - `ChromaticAberration offset={new THREE.Vector2(0.002, 0.002)}` also created new object on every render
     - Fix: extracted `EffectComposer` into `PostProcessing = memo(...)` component + downgraded library + removed v3-only props:
     ```tsx
     const PostProcessing = memo(function PostProcessing() {
       const chromaticOffset = useMemo(() => new THREE.Vector2(0.002, 0.002), []);
       return (
         <EffectComposer>
           <Bloom intensity={2.8} luminanceThreshold={0.1} luminanceSmoothing={0.7} mipmapBlur />
           <ChromaticAberration offset={chromaticOffset} />
           <Noise opacity={0.12} />
           <Vignette eskil={false} offset={0.05} darkness={1.4} />
         </EffectComposer>
       );
     });
     ```
     - Inside `Scene` component, replaced the inline `<EffectComposer>` block with `<PostProcessing />`

   - **`package.json` + `bun.lock`**
     - Downgraded `@react-three/postprocessing` from `^3.0.4` to `2.19.1`
     - v2.x peerDependency: `@react-three/fiber >= 6.0` (compatible with v8.18)
     - v3.x peerDependency: `@react-three/fiber ^9.0.0` (incompatible with v8.18)

   - **`node_modules/@react-three/fiber/dist/events-d0566a2e.cjs.dev.js`** (read-only investigation)
     - `prepare()` function shows `__r3f` initialized with `objects: []` NOT `children`
     - `appendChild` uses `parentInstance.__r3f.objects.push(child)` for non-Object3D children
     - Confirmed the incompatibility with postprocessing v3

   - **`node_modules/@react-three/postprocessing/src/EffectComposer.tsx`** (read-only investigation)
     - Line 138: `const children = groupInstance.children` — reads `__r3f.children` which is `undefined` in R3F v8
     - Comment: `// TODO: rewrite all of this with R3F v9` — confirms it was written for v9

   - **`HANDOFF.md`**
     - Task State section updated to mark "Constellation Bugfix" as ✅ sealed
     - Full HANDOFF.md update was in progress when `/handoff` was interrupted

4. Errors and fixes:

   - **Infinite spinner on Constellation page**:
     - Cause: `setLoaded(true)` only called when `edgesQuery.data` was truthy; query error left `loaded = false` permanently
     - Fix: use `edgesQuery.isSuccess` and `edgesQuery.isError` flags

   - **THREE.WebGLAttributes buffer resize crash**:
     - Cause: `particleCount` in `AgentEdge.tsx` changed between 24/10 when edge status changed; WebGL can't resize BufferAttribute in-place
     - Fix: `key={particleCount}` on `<points>` element forces full remount

   - **Font 404 error**:
     - Cause: `font="/fonts/Inter-Bold.woff"` in `SquadHull.tsx` referenced non-existent file
     - Fix: removed `font` prop entirely

   - **`TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'length')`** (main crash):
     - Initial wrong hypothesis: `React.memo` on `EffectComposer` to prevent re-renders
     - That fix deployed but crash persisted — user confirmed "Ainda não vejo a pagina de constelação"
     - Root cause found: version incompatibility — `@react-three/postprocessing` v3.0.4 reads `__r3f.children` (R3F v9 API) but R3F v8.18 uses `__r3f.objects` → `undefined.length` crash
     - Fix: downgrade to `@react-three/postprocessing@2.19.1` (R3F v8 compatible)

   - **`WebGLRenderer: Context Lost`**:
     - Secondary consequence of the crash loop running at 60fps
     - Resolved by fixing the root crash

   - **`radialModulation`/`modulationOffset` props incompatibility**:
     - These props are v3 API for `ChromaticAberration`; not available in v2.x
     - Fix: removed both props, keeping only `offset`

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Identified 4 distinct bugs** in the Constellation page through systematic log analysis
   - **Traced `__r3f.children` = undefined** by reading R3F v8's actual compiled reconciler source, discovering `objects` vs `children` naming difference between v8 and v9
   - **Verified fix** by confirming `__r3f.children` count in new build bundle = 0
   - **Two commits** cleanly separate the fixes by category

> 204	     ↓ (status changes sync to ReactFlow via useEffect)
> 205	ReactFlow renders visual status (idle/running/done/error/blocked)
> 206	```
